{\n   276→\tweb.Env = cfg.Env\n   277→\tm := web.New()\n   278→\n   279→\ths := &HTTPServer{\n   280→\t\tCfg:                          cfg,\n   281→\t\tRouteRegister:                routeRegister,\n            m,\n   327→\t\tListener:                     opts.Listener,\n   328→\t\tSocialService:                socialService,\n   329→\t\tEncryptionService:            encryptionService,\n     publicDashboardsApi,\n   358→\t\tuserService:                  userService,\n   359→\t\ttempUserService:              tempUserService,\n   360→\t\tloginAttemptService:          loginAttemptService,\n   361→\t\torgService:                   orgService,\n   362→\t\tTeamService:                  teamService,\n   363→\t\tnavTreeService:               navTreeService,\n   364→\t\taccesscontrolService:         accesscontrolService,\n   365→\t\tannotationsRepo:              annotationRepo,\n   366→\t\ttagService:                   tagService,\n   367→\t\toauthTokenService:            oauthTokenService,\n   368→\t\tstatsService:                 statsService,\n   369→\t\tauthnService:                 authnService,\n   370→\t\tpluginsCDNService:            pluginsCDNService,\n   371→\t\tmanagedPluginsService:        managedPlugins,\n   372→\t\tstarApi:                      starApi,\n   373→\t\tpromRegister:                 promRegister,\n 377→\t\tanonService:                  anonService,\n   378→\t\tuserVerifier:                 userVerifier,\n   379→\t}\n   380→\tif hs.Listener != nil {\n   381→\t\ths.log.Debug(\"Using provided listener\")\n   382→\t}\n   383→\ths.registerRoutes()\n   384→\n   385→\t// Register access control scope resolver for annotations\n   386→\ths.AccessControl.RegisterScopeAttributeResolver(AnnotationTypeScopeResolver(hs.annotationsRepo, features, dashboardService, folderStore))\n   387→\n   388→\tif err := hs.declareFixedRoles(); err != nil {\n   389→\t\treturn nil, err\n   390→\t}\n   391→\treturn hs, nil\n   392→}\n   393→\n   394→func (hs *HTTPServer) AddMiddleware(middleware web.Handler) {\n   395→\ths.middlewares = append(hs.middlewares, middleware)\n   396→}\n   397→\n   398→func (hs *HTTPServer) AddNamedMiddleware(middleware routing.RegisterNamedMiddleware) {\n   399→\ths.namedMiddlewares = append(hs.namedMiddlewares, middleware)\n   400→}\n   401→\n   402→func (hs *HTTPServer) Run(ctx context.Context) error {\n   403→\ths.context = ctx\n   404→\n   405→\ths.applyRoutes()\n   406→\n   407→\t// Remove any square brackets enclosing IPv6 addresses, a format we support for backwards compatibility\n   408→\thost := strings.TrimSuffix(strings.TrimPrefix(hs.Cfg.HTTPAddr, \"[\"), \"]\")\n   409→\ths.httpSrv = &http.Server{\n   410→\t\tAddr:        net.JoinHostPort(host, hs.Cfg.HTTPPort),\n   411→\t\tHandler:     hs.web,\n   412→\t\tReadTimeout: hs.Cfg.ReadTimeout,\n   413→\t}\n   414→\tswitch hs.Cfg.Protocol {\n   415→\tcase setting.HTTP2Scheme, setting.HTTPSScheme:\n   416→\t\tif err := hs.configureTLS(); err != nil {\n   417→\t\t\treturn err\n   418→\t\t}\n   419→\t\tif hs.Cfg.CertFile != \"\" && hs.Cfg.KeyFile != \"\" {\n   420→\t\t\tif hs.Cfg.CertWatchInterval > 0 {\n   421→\t\t\t\ths.httpSrv.TLSConfig.GetCertificate = hs.GetCertificate\n   422→\t\t\t\tgo hs.WatchAndUpdateCerts(ctx)\n   423→\t\t\t\ths.log.Debug(\"HTTP Server certificates reload feature is enabled\")\n   424→\t\t\t} else {\n   425→\t\t\t\ths.log.Debug(\"HTTP Server certificates reload feature is NOT enabled\")\n   426→\t\t\t}\n   427→\t\t}\n   428→\tdefault:\n   429→\t}\n   430→\n   431→\tlistener, err := hs.getListener()\n   432→\tif err != nil {\n   433→\t\treturn err\n   434→\t}\n   435→\n   436→\ths.log.Info(\"HTTP Server Listen\", \"address\", listener.Addr().String(), \"protocol\",\n   437→\t\ths.Cfg.Protocol, \"subUrl\", hs.Cfg.AppSubURL, \"socket\", hs.Cfg.SocketPath)\n   438→\n   439→\tvar wg sync.WaitGroup\n   440→\twg.Add(1)\n   441→\n   442→\t// handle http shutdown on server context done\n   443→\tgo func() {\n   444→\t\tdefer wg.Done()\n   445→\n   446→\t\t<-ctx.Done()\n   447→\t\tif err := hs.httpSrv.Shutdown(context.Background()); err != nil {\n   448→\t\t\ths.log.Error(\"Failed to shutdown server\", \"error\", err)\n   449→\t\t}\n   450→\t}()\n   451→\n   452→\tswitch hs.Cfg.Protocol {\n   453→\tcase setting.HTTPScheme, setting.SocketScheme:\n   454→\t\tif err := hs.httpSrv.Serve(listener); err != nil {\n   455→\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {\n   456→\t\t\t\ths.log.Debug(\"server was shutdown gracefully\")\n   457→\t\t\t\treturn nil\n   458→\t\t\t}\n   459→\t\t\treturn err\n   460→\t\t}\n   461→\tcase setting.HTTP2Scheme, setting.HTTPSScheme:\n   462→\t\tif err := hs.httpSrv.ServeTLS(listener, \"\", \"\"); err != nil {\n   463→\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {\n   464→\t\t\t\ths.log.Debug(\"server was shutdown gracefully\")\n   465→\t\t\t\treturn nil\n   466→\t\t\t}\n   467→\t\t\treturn err\n   468→\t\t}\n   469→\tdefault:\n   470→\t\tpanic(fmt.Sprintf(\"Unhandled protocol %q\", hs.Cfg.Protocol))\n   471→\t}\n   472→\n   473→\twg.Wait()\n   474→\n   475→\treturn nil\n   476→}\n   477→\n   478→func (hs *HTTPServer) getListener() (net.Listener, error) {\n   479→\tif hs.Listener != nil {\n   480→\t\treturn hs.Listener, nil\n   481→\t}\n   482→\n   483→\tswitch hs.Cfg.Protocol {\n   484→\tcase setting.HTTPScheme, setting.HTTPSScheme, setting.HTTP2Scheme:\n   485→\t\tlistener, err := net.Listen(\"tcp\", hs.httpSrv.Addr)\n   486→\t\tif err != nil {\n   487→\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open listener on address %s: %w\", hs.httpSrv.Addr, err)\n   488→\t\t}\n   489→\t\treturn listener, nil\n   490→\tcase setting.SocketScheme:\n   491→\t\tlistener, err := net.ListenUnix(\"unix\", &net.UnixAddr{Name: hs.Cfg.SocketPath, Net: \"unix\"})\n   492→\t\tif err != nil {\n   493→\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open listener for socket %s: %w\", hs.Cfg.SocketPath, err)\n   494→\t\t}\n   495→\n   496→\t\t// Make socket writable by group\n   497→\t\t// nolint:gosec\n   498→\t\tif err := os.Chmod(hs.Cfg.SocketPath, os.FileMode(hs.Cfg.SocketMode)); err != nil {\n   499→\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to change socket mode %d: %w\", hs.Cfg.SocketMode, err)\n   500→\t\t}\n   501→\n   502→\t\t// golang.org/pkg/os does not have chgrp\n   503→\t\t// Changing the gid of a file without privileges requires that the target group is in the group of the process and that the process is the file owner\n   504→\t\tif err := os.Chown(hs.Cfg.SocketPath, -1, hs.Cfg.SocketGid); err != nil {\n   505→\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to change socket group id %d: %w\", hs.Cfg.SocketGid, err)\n   506→\t\t}\n   507→\n   508→\t\treturn listener, nil\n   509→\tdefault:\n   510→\t\ths.log.Error(\"Invalid protocol\", \"protocol\", hs.Cfg.Protocol)\n   511→\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid protocol %q\", hs.Cfg.Protocol)\n   512→\t}\n   513→}\n   514→\n   515→func (hs *HTTPServer) selfSignedCert() ([]tls.Certificate, error) {\n   516→\ttemplate := &x509.Certificate{\n   517→\t\tIsCA:                  true,\n   518→\t\tBasicConstraintsValid: true,\n   519→\t\tSubjectKeyId:          []byte{1},\n   520→\t\tSerialNumber:          big.NewInt(1),\n   521→\t\tSubject: pkix.Name{\n   522→\t\t\tCommonName: hs.Cfg.Domain,\n   523→\t\t},\n   524→\t\tNotBefore: time.Now(),\n   525→\t\tNotAfter:  time.Now().AddDate(1, 0, 0),\n   526→\t\t// see http://golang.org/pkg/crypto/x509/#KeyUsage\n   527→\t\tExtKeyUsage: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth, x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth},\n   528→\t\tKeyUsage:    x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature | x509.KeyUsageCertSign,\n   529→\t}\n   530→\n   531→\t// generate private key\n   532→\tprivatekey, err := rsa.GenerateKey(rand.Reader, 4096)\n   533→\tif err != nil {\n   534→\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"error generating tls private key: %w\", err)\n   535→\t}\n   536→\n   537→\tpublickey := &privatekey.PublicKey\n   538→\n   539→\t// create a self-signed certificate\n   540→\tvar parent = template\n   541→\tcertBytes, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, template, parent, publickey, privatekey)\n   542→\tif err != nil {\n   543→\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"error generating tls self-signed certificate: %w\", err)\n   544→\t}\n   545→\n   546→\t// encode certificate and private key to PEM\n   547→\tcertPEM := new(bytes.Buffer)\n   548→\t_ = pem.Encode(certPEM, &pem.Block{\n   549→\t\tType:  \"CERTIFICATE\",\n   550→\t\tBytes: certBytes,\n   551→\t})\n   552→\n   553→\tcertPrivKeyPEM := new(bytes.Buffer)\n   554→\t_ = pem.Encode(certPrivKeyPEM, &pem.Block{\n   555→\t\tType:  \"RSA PRIVATE KEY\",\n   556→\t\tBytes: x509.MarshalPKCS1PrivateKey(privatekey),\n   557→\t})\n   558→\n   559→\t// create tlsCertificate from generated certificate and private key\n   560→\ttlsCert, err := tls.X509KeyPair(certPEM.Bytes(), certPrivKeyPEM.Bytes())\n   561→\tif err != nil {\n   562→\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"error creating tls self-signed certificate: %w\", err)\n   563→\t}\n   564→\n   565→\treturn []tls.Certificate{tlsCert}, nil\n   566→}\n   567→\n   568→func (hs *HTTPServer) tlsCertificates() ([]tls.Certificate, error) {\n   569→\t// if we don't have either a cert or key specified, generate a self-signed certificate\n   570→\tif hs.Cfg.CertFile == \"\" && hs.Cfg.KeyFile == \"\" {\n   571→\t\treturn hs.selfSignedCert()\n   572→\t}\n   573→\n   574→\ttlsCert, err := hs.readCertificates()\n   575→\tif err != nil {\n   576→\t\treturn nil, err\n   577→\t}\n   578→\ths.tlsCerts.certs = tlsCert\n   579→\n   580→\tif err := hs.updateMtimeOfServerCerts(); err != nil {\n   581→\t\treturn nil, err\n   582→\t}\n   583→\treturn []tls.Certificate{*tlsCert}, nil\n   584→}\n   585→\n   586→func (hs *HTTPServer) applyRoutes() {\n   587→\t// start with middlewares & static routes\n   588→\ths.addMiddlewaresAndStaticRoutes()\n   589→\t// then add view routes & api routes\n   590→\ths.RouteRegister.Register(hs.web, hs.namedMiddlewares...)\n   591→\t// lastly not found route\n   592→\ths.web.NotFound(middleware.ProvideRouteOperationName(\"notfound\"), middleware.ReqSignedIn, hs.NotFoundHandler)\n   593→}\n   594→\n   595→func (hs *HTTPServer) addMiddlewaresAndStaticRoutes() {\n   596→\tm := hs.web\n   597→\n   598→\tm.Use(requestmeta.SetupRequestMetadata())\n   599→\tm.Use(middleware.RequestTracing(hs.tracer))\n   600→\tm.Use(middleware.RequestMetrics(hs.Features, hs.Cfg, hs.promRegister))\n   601→\n   602→\tm.UseMiddleware(hs.LoggerMiddleware.Middleware())\n   603→\n   604→\tif hs.Cfg.EnableGzip {\n   605→\t\tm.UseMiddleware(middleware.Gziper())\n   606→\t}\n   607→\n   608→\tm.UseMiddleware(middleware.Recovery(hs.Cfg, hs.License))\n   609→\tm.UseMiddleware(hs.Csrf.Middleware())\n   610→\n   611→\ths.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, \"build\", \"public/build\")\n   612→\ths.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, \"\", \"public\", \"/public/views/swagger.html\")\n   613→\ths.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, \"robots.txt\", \"robots.txt\")\n   614→\ths.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, \"mockServiceWorker.js\", \"mockServiceWorker.js\")\n   615→\n   616→\tif hs.Cfg.ImageUploadProvider == \"local\" {\n   617→\t\ths.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.ImagesDir, \"\", \"/public/img/attachments\")\n   618→\t}\n   619→\n   620→\tif len(hs.Cfg.CustomResponseHeaders) > 0 {\n   621→\t\tm.Use(middleware.AddCustomResponseHeaders(hs.Cfg))\n   622→\t}\n   623→\n   624→\tm.Use(middleware.AddDefaultResponseHeaders(hs.Cfg))\n   625→\n   626→\tif hs.Cfg.ServeFromSubPath && hs.Cfg.AppSubURL != \"\" {\n   627→\t\tm.SetURLPrefix(hs.Cfg.AppSubURL)\n   628→\t\tm.UseMiddleware(middleware.SubPathRedirect(hs.Cfg))\n   629→\t}\n   630→\n   631→\tm.UseMiddleware(web.Renderer(filepath.Join(hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, \"views\"), \"[[\", \"]]\"))\n   632→\n   633→\t// These endpoints are used for monitoring the Grafana instance\n   634→\t// and should not be redirected or rejected.\n   635→\tm.Use(hs.healthzHandler)\n   636→\tm.Use(hs.apiHealthHandler)\n   637→\tm.Use(hs.metricsEndpoint)\n   638→\tm.Use(hs.pluginMetricsEndpoint)\n   639→\tm.Use(hs.frontendLogEndpoints())\n   640→\n   641→\tm.UseMiddleware(hs.ContextHandler.Middleware)\n   642→\tm.Use(middleware.OrgRedirect(hs.Cfg, hs.userService))\n   643→\n   644→\t// needs to be after context handler\n   645→\tif hs.Cfg.EnforceDomain {\n   646→\t\tm.Use(middleware.ValidateHostHeader(hs.Cfg))\n   647→\t}\n   648→\t// handle action urls\n   649→\tm.UseMiddleware(middleware.ValidateActionUrl(hs.Cfg, hs.log))\n   650→\n   651→\tm.Use(middleware.HandleNoCacheHeaders)\n   652→\n   653→\tif hs.Cfg.CSPEnabled || hs.Cfg.CSPReportOnlyEnabled {\n   654→\t\tm.UseMiddleware(middleware.ContentSecurityPolicy(hs.Cfg, hs.log))\n   655→\t}\n   656→\n   657→\tfor _, mw := range hs.middlewares {\n   658→\t\tm.Use(mw)\n   659→\t}\n   660→}\n   661→\n   662→func (hs *HTTPServer) metricsEndpoint(ctx *web.Context) {\n   663→\tif !hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointEnabled {\n   664→\t\treturn\n   665→\t}\n   666→\n   667→\tif ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet || ctx.Req.URL.Path != \"/metrics\" {\n   668→\t\treturn\n   669→\t}\n   670→\n   671→\tif hs.metricsEndpointBasicAuthEnabled() && !BasicAuthenticatedRequest(ctx.Req, hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointBasicAuthUsername, hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointBasicAuthPassword) {\n   672→\t\tctx.Resp.Header().Set(\"WWW-Authenticate\", `Basic realm=\"Grafana\"`)\n   673→\t\tctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusUnauthorized)\n   674→\t\treturn\n   675→\t}\n   676→\n   677→\tpromhttp.\n   678→\t\tHandlerFor(hs.promGatherer, promhttp.HandlerOpts{EnableOpenMetrics: true}).\n   679→\t\tServeHTTP(ctx.Resp, ctx.Req)\n   680→}\n   681→\n   682→// healthzHandler always return 200 - Ok if Grafana's web server is running\n   683→func (hs *HTTPServer) healthzHandler(ctx *web.Context) {\n   684→\tnotHeadOrGet := ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet && ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodHead\n   685→\tif notHeadOrGet || ctx.Req.URL.Path != \"/healthz\" {\n   686→\t\treturn\n   687→\t}\n   688→\n   689→\tctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)\n   690→\tif _, err := ctx.Resp.Write([]byte(\"Ok\")); err != nil {\n   691→\t\ths.log.Error(\"could not write to response\", \"err\", err)\n   692→\t}\n   693→}\n   694→\n   695→// swagger:model healthResponse\n   696→type healthResponse struct {\n   697→\tDatabase         string `json:\"database\"`\n   698→\tVersion          string `json:\"version,omitempty\"`\n   699→\tCommit           string `json:\"commit,omitempty\"`\n   700→\tEnterpriseCommit string `json:\"enterpriseCommit,omitempty\"`\n   701→}\n   702→\n   703→// swagger:route GET /health health getHealth\n   704→//\n   705→// apiHealthHandler will return ok if Grafana's web server is running and it\n   706→// can access the database. If the database cannot be accessed it will return\n   707→// http status code 503.\n   708→//\n   709→// Responses:\n   710→// 200: healthResponse\n   711→// 503: internalServerError\n   712→func (hs *HTTPServer) apiHealthHandler(ctx *web.Context) {\n   713→\tnotHeadOrGet := ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet && ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodHead\n   714→\tif notHeadOrGet || ctx.Req.URL.Path != \"/api/health\" {\n   715→\t\treturn\n   716→\t}\n   717→\n   718→\tdata := healthResponse{\n   719→\t\tDatabase: \"ok\",\n   720→\t}\n   721→\tif !hs.Cfg.AnonymousHideVersion {\n   722→\t\tdata.Version = hs.Cfg.BuildVersion\n   723→\t\tdata.Commit = hs.Cfg.BuildCommit\n   724→\t\tif hs.Cfg.EnterpriseBuildCommit != \"NA\" && hs.Cfg.EnterpriseBuildCommit != \"\" {\n   725→\t\t\tdata.EnterpriseCommit = hs.Cfg.EnterpriseBuildCommit\n   726→\t\t}\n   727→\t}\n   728→\n   729→\tif !hs.databaseHealthy(ctx.Req.Context()) {\n   730→\t\tdata.Database = \"failing\"\n   731→\t\tctx.Resp.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json; charset=UTF-8\")\n   732→\t\tctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n   733→\t} else {\n   734→\t\tctx.Resp.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json; charset=UTF-8\")\n   735→\t\tctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)\n   736→\t}\n   737→\n   738→\tdataBytes, err :=     pluginStore,\n\t\tpluginStaticRouteResolver:    pluginStaticRouteResolver,\n\t\tpluginDashboardService:       pluginDashboardService,\n\t\tpluginAssets:        queryHistoryService,\n\t\tCorrelationsService:          correlationsService,\n\t\tFeatures:                     features, // a read only view of the managers state\n\t\tStorageService:               storageService,\n\t\tRemoteCacheService:           remoteCache,\n\t\tProvisioningService:          provisioningService,\n\t\tAccessControl:                accessControl,\n\t\tDataProxy:                    dataSourceProxy,\n\t\tSearchV2HTTPService:          searchv2HTTPService,\n\t\tUnifiedSearchHTTPService:     unifiedSearchHTTPService,\n\t\tSearchService:                searchService,\n\t\tLive:                         live,\n\t\tLivePushGateway:              livePushGateway,\n\t\tpluginContextProvider:        plugCtxProvider,\n\t\tContextHandler:               contextHandler,\n\t\tLoggerMiddleware:             loggerMiddleware,\n\t\tAlertNG:                      alertNG,\n\t\tLibraryPanelService:          libraryPanelService,\n\t\tLibraryElementService:        libraryElementService,\n\t\tQuotaService:                 quotaService,\n\t\ttracer:                       tracer,\n\t\tlog:                          log.New(\"http.server\"),\n\t\tweb:                          m,\n\t\tListener:                     opts.Listener,\n\t\tSocialService:                socialService,\n\t\tEncryptionService:            encryptionService,\n\t\tSecretsService:               secretsService,\n\t\tsecretsPluginManager:         secretsPluginManager,\n\t\tsecretsMigrator:              secretsMigrator,\n\t\tsecretsPluginMigrator:        secretsPluginMigrator,\n\t\tsecretsStore:                 secretsStore,\n\t\tDataSourcesService:           dataSourcesService,\n\t\tsearchUsersService: authInfoService,\n\t\tNotificationService:          notificationService,\n\t\tDashboardService:             dashboardService,\n\t\tdashboardProvisioningService:                     csrfService,\n\t\tfolderPermissionsService:     folderPermissionsService,\n\t\tdashboardPermissionsService:  dashboardPermissionsService,\n\t\tdashboardVersionService:      dashboardVersionService,\n\t\tstarService:                  starService,\n\t\tplaylistService:              playlistService,\n\t\tapiKeyService:                apiKeyService,\n\t\tkvStore:                      kvStore,\n\t\tPublicDashboardsApi:          publicDashboardsApi,\n\t\tuserService:                  userService,\n\t\ttempUserService:              tempUserService,\n\t\tloginAttemptService:          loginAttemptService,\n\t\torgService:                   orgService,\n\t\tTeamService:                  teamService,\n\t\tnavTreeService:               navTreeService,\n\t\taccesscontrolService:         accesscontrolService,\n\t\tannotationsRepo:              annotationRepo,\n\t\ttagService:                   tagService,\n\t\toauthTokenService:            oauthTokenService,\n\t\tstatsService:                 statsService,\n\t\tauthnService:                 authnService,\n\t\tpluginsCDNService:            pluginsCDNService,\n\t\tmanagedPluginsService:        managedPlugins,\n\t\tstarApi:                      starApi,\n\t\tpromRegister:                 promRegister,\n\t\tpromGatherer:                 promGatherer,\n\t\tclientConfigProvider:         clientConfigProvider,\n\t\tnamespacer:                   request.GetNamespaceMapper(cfg),\n\t\tanonService:                  anonService,\n\t\tuserVerifier:                 userVerifier,\n\t}\n\tif hs.Listener != nil {\n\t\ths.log.Debug(\"Using provided listener\")\n\t}\n\ths.registerRoutes()\n\n\t// Register access control scope resolver for annotations\n\ths.AccessControl.RegisterScopeAttributeResolver(AnnotationTypeScopeResolver(hs.annotationsRepo, features, dashboardService, folderStore))\n\n\tif err := hs.declareFixedRoles(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn hs, nil\n}\n\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) AddMiddleware(middleware web.Handler) {\n\ths.middlewares = append(hs.middlewares, middleware)\n}\n\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) AddNamedMiddleware(middleware routing.RegisterNamedMiddleware) {\n\ths.namedMiddlewares = append(hs.namedMiddlewares, middleware)\n}\n\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) Run(ctx context.Context) error {\n\ths.context = ctx\n\n\ths.applyRoutes()\n\n\t// Remove any square brackets enclosing IPv6 addresses, a format we support for backwards compatibility\n\thost := strings.TrimSuffix(strings.TrimPrefix(hs.Cfg.HTTPAddr, \"[\"), \"]\")\n\ths.httpSrv = &http.Server{\n\t\tAddr:        net.JoinHostPort(host, hs.Cfg.HTTPPort),\n\t\tHandler:     hs.web,\n\t\tReadTimeout: hs.Cfg.ReadTimeout,\n\t}\n\tswitch hs.Cfg.Protocol {\n\tcase setting.HTTP2Scheme, setting.HTTPSScheme:\n\t\tif err := hs.configureTLS(); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif hs.Cfg.CertFile != \"\" && hs.Cfg.KeyFile != \"\" {\n\t\t\tif hs.Cfg.CertWatchInterval > 0 {\n\t\t\t\ths.httpSrv.TLSConfig.GetCertificate = hs.GetCertificate\n\t\t\t\tgo hs.WatchAndUpdateCerts(ctx)\n\t\t\t\ths.log.Debug(\"HTTP Server certificates reload feature is enabled\")\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\ths.log.Debug(\"HTTP Server certificates reload feature is NOT enabled\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\tdefault:\n\t}\n\n\tlistener, err := hs.getListener()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\ths.log.Info(\"HTTP Server Listen\", \"address\", listener.Addr().String(), \"protocol\",\n\t\ths.Cfg.Protocol, \"subUrl\", hs.Cfg.AppSubURL, \"socket\", hs.Cfg.SocketPath)\n\n\tvar wg sync.WaitGroup\n\twg.Add(1)\n\n\t// handle http shutdown on server context done\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tdefer wg.Done()\n\n\t\t<-ctx.Done()\n\t\tif err := hs.httpSrv.Shutdown(context.Background()); err != nil {\n\t\t\ths.log.Error(\"Failed to shutdown server\", \"error\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\tswitch hs.Cfg.Protocol {\n\tcase setting.HTTPScheme, setting.SocketScheme:\n\t\tif err := hs.httpSrv.Serve(listener); err != nil {\n\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {\n\t\t\t\ths.log.Debug(\"server was shutdown gracefully\")\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\tcase setting.HTTP2Scheme, setting.HTTPSScheme:\n\t\tif err := hs.httpSrv.ServeTLS(listener, \"\", \"\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {\n\t\t\t\ths.log.Debug(\"server was shutdown gracefully\")\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\tdefault:\n\t\tpanic(fmt.Sprintf(\"Unhandled protocol %q\", hs.Cfg.Protocol))\n\t}\n\n\twg.Wait()\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) getListener() (net.Listener, error) {\n\tif hs.Listener != nil {\n\t\treturn hs.Listener, nil\n\t}\n\n\tswitch hs.Cfg.Protocol {\n\tcase setting.HTTPScheme, setting.HTTPSScheme, setting.HTTP2Scheme:\n\t\tlistener, err := net.Listen(\"tcp\", hs.httpSrv.Addr)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open listener on address %s: %w\", hs.httpSrv.Addr, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn listener, nil\n\tcase setting.SocketScheme:\n\t\tlistener, err := net.ListenUnix(\"unix\", &net.UnixAddr{Name: hs.Cfg.SocketPath, Net: \"unix\"})\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open listener for socket %s: %w\", hs.Cfg.SocketPath, err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Make socket writable by group\n\t\t// nolint:gosec\n\t\tif err := os.Chmod(hs.Cfg.SocketPath, os.FileMode(hs.Cfg.SocketMode)); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to change socket mode %d: %w\", hs.Cfg.SocketMode, err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// golang.org/pkg/os does not have chgrp\n\t\t// Changing the gid of a file without privileges requires that the target group is in the group of the process and that the process is the file owner\n\t\tif err := os.Chown(hs.Cfg.SocketPath, -1, hs.Cfg.SocketGid); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to change socket group id %d: %w\", hs.Cfg.SocketGid, err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn listener, nil\n\tdefault:\n\t\ths.log.Error(\"Invalid protocol\", \"protocol\", hs.Cfg.Protocol)\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid protocol %q\", hs.Cfg.Protocol)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) selfSignedCert() ([]tls.Certificate, error) {\n\ttemplate := &x509.Certificate{\n\t\tIsCA:                  true,\n\t\tBasicConstraintsValid: true,\n\t\tSubjectKeyId:          []byte{1},\n\t\tSerialNumber:          big.NewInt(1),\n\t\tSubject: pkix.Name{\n\t\t\tCommonName: hs.Cfg.Domain,\n\t\t},\n\t\tNotBefore: time.Now(),\n\t\tNotAfter:  time.Now().AddDate(1, 0, 0),\n\t\t// see http://golang.org/pkg/crypto/x509/#KeyUsage\n\t\tExtKeyUsage: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth, x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth},\n\t\tKeyUsage:    x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature | x509.KeyUsageCertSign,\n\t}\n\n\t// generate private key\n\tprivatekey, err := rsa.GenerateKey(rand.Reader, 4096)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"error generating tls private key: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tpublickey := &privatekey.PublicKey\n\n\t// create a self-signed certificate\n\tvar parent = template\n\tcertBytes, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, template, parent, publickey, privatekey)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"error generating tls self-signed certificate: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// encode certificate and private key to PEM\n\tcertPEM := new(bytes.Buffer)\n\t_ = pem.Encode(certPEM, &pem.Block{\n\t\tType:  \"CERTIFICATE\",\n\t\tBytes: certBytes,\n\t})\n\n\tcertPrivKeyPEM := new(bytes.Buffer)\n\t_ = pem.Encode(certPrivKeyPEM, &pem.Block{\n\t\tType:  \"RSA PRIVATE KEY\",\n\t\tBytes: x509.MarshalPKCS1PrivateKey(privatekey),\n\t})\n\n\t// create tlsCertificate from generated certificate and private key\n\ttlsCert, err := tls.X509KeyPair(certPEM.Bytes(), certPrivKeyPEM.Bytes())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"error creating tls self-signed certificate: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn []tls.Certificate{tlsCert}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) tlsCertificates() ([]tls.Certificate, error) {\n\t// if we don't have either a cert or key specified, generate a self-signed certificate\n\tif hs.Cfg.CertFile == \"\" && hs.Cfg.KeyFile == \"\" {\n\t\treturn hs.selfSignedCert()\n\t}\n\n\ttlsCert, err := hs.readCertificates()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\ths.tlsCerts.certs = tlsCert\n\n\tif err := hs.updateMtimeOfServerCerts(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn []tls.Certificate{*tlsCert}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) applyRoutes() {\n\t// start with middlewares & static routes\n\ths.addMiddlewaresAndStaticRoutes()\n\t// then add view routes & api routes\n\ths.RouteRegister.Register(hs.web, hs.namedMiddlewares...)\n\t// lastly not found route\n\ths.web.NotFound(middleware.ProvideRouteOperationName(\"notfound\"), middleware.ReqSignedIn, hs.NotFoundHandler)\n}\n\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) addMiddlewaresAndStaticRoutes() {\n\tm := hs.web\n\n\tm.Use(requestmeta.SetupRequestMetadata())\n\tm.Use(middleware.RequestTracing(hs.tracer))\n\tm.Use(middleware.RequestMetrics(hs.Features, hs.Cfg, hs.promRegister))\n\n\tm.UseMiddleware(hs.LoggerMiddleware.Middleware())\n\n\tif hs.Cfg.EnableGzip {\n\t\tm.UseMiddleware(middleware.Gziper())\n\t}\n\n\tm.UseMiddleware(middleware.Recovery(hs.Cfg, hs.License))\n\tm.UseMiddleware(hs.Csrf.Middleware())\n\n\ths.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, \"build\", \"public/build\")\n\ths.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, \"\", \"public\", \"/public/views/swagger.html\")\n\ths.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, \"robots.txt\", \"robots.txt\")\n\ths.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, \"mockServiceWorker.js\", \"mockServiceWorker.js\")\n\n\tif hs.Cfg.ImageUploadProvider == \"local\" {\n\t\ths.mapStatic(m, hs.Cfg.ImagesDir, \"\", \"/public/img/attachments\")\n\t}\n\n\tif len(hs.Cfg.CustomResponseHeaders) > 0 {\n\t\tm.Use(middleware.AddCustomResponseHeaders(hs.Cfg))\n\t}\n\n\tm.Use(middleware.AddDefaultResponseHeaders(hs.Cfg))\n\n\tif hs.Cfg.ServeFromSubPath && hs.Cfg.AppSubURL != \"\" {\n\t\tm.SetURLPrefix(hs.Cfg.AppSubURL)\n\t\tm.UseMiddleware(middleware.SubPathRedirect(hs.Cfg))\n\t}\n\n\tm.UseMiddleware(web.Renderer(filepath.Join(hs.Cfg.StaticRootPath, \"views\"), \"[[\", \"]]\"))\n\n\t// These endpoints are used for monitoring the Grafana instance\n\t// and should not be redirected or rejected.\n\tm.Use(hs.healthzHandler)\n\tm.Use(hs.apiHealthHandler)\n\tm.Use(hs.metricsEndpoint)\n\tm.Use(hs.pluginMetricsEndpoint)\n\tm.Use(hs.frontendLogEndpoints())\n\n\tm.UseMiddleware(hs.ContextHandler.Middleware)\n\tm.Use(middleware.OrgRedirect(hs.Cfg, hs.userService))\n\n\t// needs to be after context handler\n\tif hs.Cfg.EnforceDomain {\n\t\tm.Use(middleware.ValidateHostHeader(hs.Cfg))\n\t}\n\t// handle action urls\n\tm.UseMiddleware(middleware.ValidateActionUrl(hs.Cfg, hs.log))\n\n\tm.Use(middleware.HandleNoCacheHeaders)\n\n\tif hs.Cfg.CSPEnabled || hs.Cfg.CSPReportOnlyEnabled {\n\t\tm.UseMiddleware(middleware.ContentSecurityPolicy(hs.Cfg, hs.log))\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, mw := range hs.middlewares {\n\t\tm.Use(mw)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) metricsEndpoint(ctx *web.Context) {\n\tif !hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointEnabled {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet || ctx.Req.URL.Path != \"/metrics\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif hs.metricsEndpointBasicAuthEnabled() && !BasicAuthenticatedRequest(ctx.Req, hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointBasicAuthUsername, hs.Cfg.MetricsEndpointBasicAuthPassword) {\n\t\tctx.Resp.Header().Set(\"WWW-Authenticate\", `Basic realm=\"Grafana\"`)\n\t\tctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusUnauthorized)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tpromhttp.\n\t\tHandlerFor(hs.promGatherer, promhttp.HandlerOpts{EnableOpenMetrics: true}).\n\t\tServeHTTP(ctx.Resp, ctx.Req)\n}\n\n// healthzHandler always return 200 - Ok if Grafana's web server is running\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) healthzHandler(ctx *web.Context) {\n\tnotHeadOrGet := ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet && ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodHead\n\tif notHeadOrGet || ctx.Req.URL.Path != \"/healthz\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)\n\tif _, err := ctx.Resp.Write([]byte(\"Ok\")); err != nil {\n\t\ths.log.Error(\"could not write to response\", \"err\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\n// swagger:model healthResponse\ntype healthResponse struct {\n\tDatabase         string `json:\"database\"`\n\tVersion          string `json:\"version,omitempty\"`\n\tCommit           string `json:\"commit,omitempty\"`\n\tEnterpriseCommit string `json:\"enterpriseCommit,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// swagger:route GET /health health getHealth\n//\n// apiHealthHandler will return ok if Grafana's web server is running and it\n// can access the database. If the database cannot be accessed it will return\n// http status code 503.\n//\n// Responses:\n// 200: healthResponse\n// 503: internalServerError\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) apiHealthHandler(ctx *web.Context) {\n\tnotHeadOrGet := ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodGet && ctx.Req.Method != http.MethodHead\n\tif notHeadOrGet || ctx.Req.URL.Path != \"/api/health\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tdata := healthResponse{\n\t\tDatabase: \"ok\",\n\t}\n\tif !hs.Cfg.AnonymousHideVersion {\n\t\tdata.Version = hs.Cfg.BuildVersion\n\t\tdata.Commit = hs.Cfg.BuildCommit\n\t\tif hs.Cfg.EnterpriseBuildCommit != \"NA\" && hs.Cfg.EnterpriseBuildCommit != \"\" {\n\t\t\tdata.EnterpriseCommit = hs.Cfg.EnterpriseBuildCommit\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif !hs.databaseHealthy(ctx.Req.Context()) {\n\t\tdata.Database = \"failing\"\n\t\tctx.Resp.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json; charset=UTF-8\")\n\t\tctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n\t} else {\n\t\tctx.Resp.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json; charset=UTF-8\")\n\t\tctx.Resp.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)\n\t}\n\n\tdataBytes, err := json.MarshalIndent(data, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\ths.log.Error(\"Failed to encode data\", \"err\", err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif _, err := ctx.Resp.Write(dataBytes); err != nil {\n\t\ths.log.Error(\"Failed to write to response\", \"err\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (hs *HTTPServer) mapStatic(m *web.Mux, rootDir string, dir string, prefix string, exclude ...string) {\n\theaders := func(c *web.Context) {\n\t\tc.Resp.Header().Set(\"Cache-Control\", \"public, max-age=3600\")\n\t}\n\n\tif prefix == \"public/build\" {\n\t\theaders = func(c *web.Context) {\n\t\t\tc.Resp.Header().Set(\"Cache-Control\", different organizational approaches.\\n\\n**Kubernetes Architecture:**\\nKubernetes uses a sophisticated registry-based architecture with version-aware metric management. The core is `KubeRegistry` interface (staging/src/k8s.io/component-base/metrics/registry.go), which wraps prometheus.Registry and adds stability tracking, deprecation handling, and metric versioning. The HTTP exposition is handled by `HandlerFor()` and `HandlerWithReset()` functions in staging/src/k8s.io/component-base/metrics/http.go that use promhttp.HandlerFor internally.\\n\\nThe `/metrics` HTTP endpoint is configured at the API server level in staging/src/k8s.io/apiserver/pkg/server/routes/metrics.go through `DefaultMetrics` and `MetricsWithReset` route handlers. These install handlers via `legacyregistry.Handler()` from staging/src/k8s.io/component-base/metrics/legacyregistry/registry.go, which provides a global registry with handlers that are already instrumented with InstrumentMetricHandler.\\n\\nKubernetes organizes metric collectors into specialized subdirectories under staging/src/k8s.io/component-base/metrics/prometheus/: workqueue (for queue depth and latency metrics), version (for build info), controllers, feature gates, client-go integration, and REST client metrics. Each package registers collectors using the legacyregistry.MustRegister() function, often in init() blocks. The architecture supports custom collectors via the StableCollector interface (staging/src/k8s.io/component-base/metrics/collector.go) with BaseStableCollector providing a foundation for implementing custom metric collectors with stability guarantees.\\n\\n**Grafana Architecture:**\\nGrafana takes a simpler, more direct approach to metrics exposition. The `/metrics` endpoint is handled as a middleware in the HTTP server (pkg/api/http_server.go) via the `metricsEndpoint()` function, which checks the request path and uses `promhttp.HandlerFor(hs.promGatherer, ...)` directly from the prometheus/promhttp package with EnableOpenMetrics option.\\n\\nThe metrics infrastructure is organized through dependency injection via the metrics service (pkg/infra/metrics/service.go). The `ProvideService()` function initializes metrics and calls `initMetricVars()` and `initFrontendMetrics()` which register metrics with the registerer. The service provides both `ProvideRegisterer()` and `ProvideGatherer()` functions that expose the prometheus registry interfaces.\\n\\nUniquely, Grafana combines multiple metric sources through a `multiRegistry` gatherer that merges metrics from the Kubernetes legacyregistry (for compatibility with Kubernetes metrics patterns) with prometheus.DefaultGatherer. An `addPrefixWrapper` ensures all metrics are prefixed with \\\"grafana_\\\" for namespacing, preventing collisions with system metrics.\\n\\nAll Grafana metrics are defined globally in pkg/infra/metrics/metrics.go as package-level variables (Counters, Gauges, Summaries, Histograms) and registered together in `initMetricVars()` via a single `reg.MustRegister()` call. Wire dependency injection configuration (pkg/infra/metrics/wireset.go) provides the registerer and gatherer to the HTTP server and services.\\n\\n**Key Differences:**\\n- **Kubernetes**: Hierarchical, version-aware metric registration with custom collector framework and stability levels; metrics distributed across component modules\\n- **Grafana**: Centralized metric definition and registration; single-pass initialization; uses wrapper gatherers for composition and prefixing\\n- **HTTP Setup**: Kubernetes uses a route-based handler installation pattern; Grafana uses middleware-based endpoint handling\\n- **Collector Organization**: Kubernetes structures collectors by functional domain (workqueue, version, controllers); Grafana centralizes metrics in a single package\\n- **Multi-Registry**: Grafana explicitly composes multiple gatherers; Kubernetes uses a single legacy registry with internal organization\"\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":703,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1219,"cache_read_input_tokens":68360,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1219,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0b6710c1-f5f9-4abf-8684-ec4a117de2cd","uuid":"c7098357-0a60-4d94-aa07-6bdf930ed388"}
